# PSP timing harness: plan, checked against the Two Towers lessons (2026-10-18)

Goal: time one Tanker deck (w00a) logic step on Ffm's PSP-3000 (ARK-4). That number decides the 50 Hz risk: 25 fps shown needs two
logic steps per displayed frame (40 ms for two steps).

Lessons file: `/mnt/project-files/tt_port/PS2_to_PSP_optimisation_lessons.md` (TT up to v24, hardware-measured).

## Where MGS2 stands against each lesson

| Lesson | MGS2 state |
|---|---|
| A9 FPU: R5900 rounds toward zero; PSP FCR31 `0x01000001`; x86 reference with MXCSR RZ + FTZ | **Gap found and fixed.** The x86 probe ran round-to-nearest, so `hashes_w00a_x86.txt` is a round-to-nearest reference. Now `MGS2_FPU_RZ=1` sets MXCSR RZ + FTZ (no DAZ, as the PSP with FS has none). RZ run: deterministic (two runs, same hashes), Snake on the deck at vb 25200 at the same position as round-to-nearest. New reference `boot/hashes_x86_rz.txt` (vb, EE hash, IOP hash every 100 vblanks to 25200). The PSP build sets FCR31 `0x01000001` before any guest code. |
| A10 counters: block 0xBC400000 word +0x50 powers the PSP off | No performance counters in the harness. Timing uses `sceKernelGetSystemTimeWide` only. |
| A10 exit order | HOME sets a quit flag, the step loop ends, (`sceGuSync`, `sceGuTerm` once a renderer exists), any exception handler is given back, then `sceKernelExitGame`; the callback thread does it itself after 2 s. The harness waits for a button at the end and never exits on its own. |
| A10 overclock | Not touched. The harness reports the clock it runs at (`scePowerGetCpuClockFrequency`). Running at 333 MHz through the official power call is Ffm's decision. |
| A2/A3 I-cache, code size | Register cache and 64→32 narrowing ported and exact on MGS2 (recomp/README.md). Memory access path cut to one mask (below). Next: `$23` pinned memory base (`-ffixed-23`), hot layout. |
| A4 dead save/restore removal blocked (game reads uninitialised stack) | Kept off (`RC_DSR` unset). MGS2 showed the same class of issue: a float register saved to the stack by a callee must hold the exact value, or EE RAM differs (fixed in regcache, 2026-10-18). |
| A5–A8 GE / VRAM | Not in the timing harness (headless logic). Applies to the renderer later: vertices in VRAM first. |
| A11 stubs by name | MGS2 has no presentation stubs yet; every function runs. Keep it that way for the timing. |
| C1 three references | x86 (RZ) → PPSSPP (booleans, FPU-patched) → hardware, same hashes. |
| C2 PPSSPP costs are ~2× optimistic | Only hardware timings count. |
| D "real PSP scratchpad for the guest scratchpad" | Done differently: the guest scratchpad lives at guest RAM 0x0000–0x3fff (the EE kernel area, unused under the HLE kernel), so one mask reaches it. |

## Memory access path (x86-checked, identical hashes to vb 25200)
- `GEN_MM_FAST` + `GUEST_SPR0` (gen_pre.h, ee_io.h): an access is `g_mem + (a & 0x1ffffff)` unless `a >> 28 == 1` (registers, VU memory),
  which goes to the runtime; a register store takes effect at once (`rt_store`, `rt_hww32`).
- Deck rates (vb 24000–25200): 3.3k register accesses per vblank go to the runtime; the 263k scratchpad accesses per vblank stay inline.
- `GEN_MM_CHECK` (x86 dev) reports any address the mask would get wrong (kseg1 registers, unmapped segments, scratchpad past 16 KB): none
  to vb 25200.

## Harness shape
1. EBOOT with the full boot path (ELF + init, r_title, n_title, r_tnk0, d00t, w00a), the pad script, disc files read from the memory stick
   (only the ranges the boot path reads), movies skipped as on x86.
2. Per logic step (one vblank): time from the vblank event to the next wait, `sceKernelGetSystemTimeWide`.
3. Print the EE hash every 100 vblanks to compare with `hashes_x86_rz.txt` (exactness first, then time).
4. Report steady-state deck step time (vb 24000+) separately from loading and early frames.

## Build v1 (2026-10-18)
- Sources: `psp/main_psp.c` (harness), `psp/Makefile`, `psp/ucontext.h` + `psp/psp_ctx.S` (coroutine switch for guest and IOP threads: s0..s7, fp, gp, sp, ra, f20..f31 (EABI callee-saved, checked with psp-gcc), FCR31).
- Generated code: the coverage-reduced headers (`tools/prune_cov.py`) of the ELF and the six boot-path stages (init, r_title, r_tnk0, n_title, d00t, w00a); `tools/stages_subset.py` cuts stages.h to them. Build dir `/home/claude/psp/build` (ephemeral), headers linked from `/home/claude/mgs2cut`.
- Machine changes for the PSP, all checked on x86 with hashes identical to `hashes_x86_rz.txt` to vb 25200 (with the disc pack): the 64 MB register shadow is three blocks (EE registers 64 KB, GS privileged 8 KB, a spare 64 KB reported if ever used: never in the run); the coverage table is allocated only when used; `MGS2_SIF_LOG` (0 on the PSP: the SIF packet log was ~7 lines per deck vblank); timing regions `runtime/host_time.h` (IOP + sound: `sif_vblank`, `sif_iop_run`; GS packet walk: GIF / VIF1 DMA), nothing on x86.
- PSP-only: `-Dexit=mgs2_exit -Dabort=mgs2_abort -Dgetenv=mgs2_getenv` (stops wait for X; settings from defaults, `mgs2.cfg`, `pad.txt`), guest host stacks 128 KB (x86 use ≤ 12 KB), IOP host stacks 64 KB (x86 use ≤ 8.2 KB), hash every 500 vblanks (fnv64 over 32 MB costs about a second on the PSP, outside the timing).
- Image: text 13.4 MB, bss 37.7 MB (guest RAM 32 MB, SPU RAM 2 MB, VIF0 buffer 1 MB), 51.1 MB in all, loaded at 0x08804000; the heap gets the rest of the 56 MB large-memory partition minus 1 MB (IOP RAM 2 MB and the thread stacks come from it). The log prints the heap headroom at start and end.
- Timing: a step is the host time from one guest vblank hook to the next, minus the hook's own work (hash, screen, log flush). Reported per 100-vblank window in log.txt, and for the deck (vb 24001+) as a summary plus `steps.csv` (step, IOP+sound, GS walk in µs per vblank). game = step − IOP − GS.
- Delivered to Ffm's folder `Desktop\mg2 port\MGS2T\` (EBOOT md5 81316334..., pack built there from Ffm's own files by `make_pak.py`, md5 07bd6b6b... identical to the container's). Not run on any PSP or emulator yet.

## PPSSPP runs of v1 (2026-10-18)
- Container, headless PPSSPP 1.20.4 built from Ffm's source tarball with the TT patches (`/home/claude/ppsspp_src`, ephemeral): v1 emulated about 200 ms per vblank at the title, almost all of it the virtual-clock poll scan (fixed in v2). Hashes matched `hashes_x86_rz.txt`.
- Ffm's PPSSPP 1.19.3 (v1, `log.txt` in `MGS2T`): hashes identical to the x86 RZ reference at every 500 vblanks to vb 20000, then the run stopped at vb 20136: `JALR to 00336ca8: not a translated function`. Cause: the stage d01t (loaded between d00t and w00a, at vb ~20100) was missing from the harness's stage list; the coverage run had also been built without d01t's coverage. The boot path is seven stage images: init, r_title, n_title, r_tnk0, d00t, d01t, w00a.

## Build v2 (2026-10-18/19)
- Stages: the seven above (`psp/Makefile` STAGES, `stages_subset.py`); d01t reduced with its own coverage (coverage run rebuilt with d01t under `GEN_COVER`).
- 333 MHz by default (`scePowerSetClockFrequency(333, 333, 166)`, Ffm's approval; nothing above 333 ever); `clock default` in `mgs2.cfg` keeps the boot clock.
- Exact host speedups (speed_notes.md §3) on, and over the deck range they take turns per 100 vblanks (all on, GS bulk walk off, poll scan skip off, all off), so one run times each (`MGS2_OPT_CYCLE`, default `<deck start>:100`).
- Timing regions are exclusive (a region entered inside another pauses it): IOP + sound, GS packet walk, draw pipeline (the call of 0x11da90, `dispatch.h`); logic = step − the three.
- `steps.csv`: `vb,step_us,iop_us,gs_us,dg_us,cfg,wait` (cfg = the speedup configuration index; wait = the word at 0x18db74, the cutscene logic-rate word from web_research.md).
- PPSSPP headless (container): hashes identical to the reference at vb 100–600; 23–64 ms per vblank emulated at the title at 333 MHz (PPSSPP timing is not hardware timing; only Ffm's PSP run counts).

## Build v2: memory (2026-10-19)
- The PSP's large-memory mode gives about 51 MB to the EBOOT (sandstone_review.md 2.8; PPSSPP gives more, so PPSSPP alone does not catch an overflow). v2 with every boot-path stage linked in was about 56 MB; it is now about 48 MB:
  - **Stage overlays.** Each stage image's translated code is `ovl_<stage>.bin` (psp/ovl_hdr.c + gen_s_<stage>.o linked by psp/ovl.ld at `ovl_arena` in MGS2T.elf, 0x450000 bytes, with `-R MGS2T.elf` and libgcc). boot/dispatch.h `STAGE_OVL` calls `stage_ovl_call` (main_psp.c) the first time the game calls into a stage image; it checks the header (magic, name, size, three MGS2T.elf symbol addresses, so an overlay from another build is refused), zeroes the overlay's bss, writes back and invalidates both caches, and logs the load time (d00t 3.9 MB: 420 ms in PPSSPP). `tools/stages_subset.py --ovl` writes the signature table without the code pointers. Sizes: w00a 4.30 MB, d00t 3.95 MB, d01t 2.16 MB, n_title 0.81 MB, init 0.16 MB, r_title / r_tnk0 15 KB.
  - **Host stacks from the main thread's stack** (`HOST_STACK_POOL`, runtime/kernel.h `HOST_STACK_ALLOC`, iop_host.h): a 1152 KB pool at the bottom of the main thread's stack (16 KB guard), never freed. A coroutine stack outside the thread's own block switched the console off in SandstoneDev's port. Stack use measured in PPSSPP: at most 9.8 KB per guest thread, 1.5 KB per IOP thread.
  - **SPU2 RAM in the 4 MB volatile block** (`HOST_BIG_ALLOC`, `sceKernelVolatileMemTryLock`, 0x08400000, unlocked at exit). The block is lost across sleep: do not sleep the PSP during a run.
  - **VIF0 buffer** 0x4000 words (`VIF0_BUF_WORDS`; the largest transfer is printed in the io dump).
- Copy the EBOOT and all seven `ovl_*.bin` together.

## PPSSPP full run of v2 (2026-10-19, container, headless 1.20.4, JIT)
- Boot to vb 25200 through all seven stages, every overlay loaded; all 50 hashes (every 500 vblanks) identical to `boot/hashes_x86_rz.txt`.
- PPSSPP's clock charges one cycle per instruction (a few long FPU / divide ops more) at the 333 MHz the harness sets, with no cache or stall model, so its times are a floor for the PSP, not a measurement. Deck (vb 24001–25200), speedups all on: step 395 ms, of which draw pipeline 331 ms, logic 55 ms (45–74 ms per 100-vblank window), GS walk 8 ms, IOP+sound 0.9 ms. The logic floor alone is above the 20 ms a 50 Hz step can have at 25 fps (two steps per 40 ms frame).

## Emulator profile (2026-10-19)
- The container's PPSSPP has the TT PspProf patch (per-PC instruction counts, simulated 16 KB 2-way I- and D-caches) plus `tools/ppsspp_mgs2.diff`: `PSPPROF_SWITCH=1` runs the JIT until the harness word `g_emuprof` turns non-zero, the profiled interpreter while it is, then the JIT again; the value 2 pauses counting (the caches keep running).
- Harness: `prof A B` in mgs2.cfg sets the word from vblank A to B, paused inside the draw pipeline and the GS walk (`prof A B all` counts them too). The word does nothing on the PSP.
- `tools/psp_prof.py <dump> <steps> MGS2T.elf ovl_<stage>.elf`: per function and per group, cost = instructions + 125 x misses (TT's PSP-3000 calibration).

- mgs2.cfg = v11's plus `MGS2_VFPU_STAT=1`: hash lines end with " vfb N", the VFPU group fallbacks so far (x86 reference `prof/hashes_x86_w01a_vfpu_vfb.txt`; emulator equal at vb 500-1500).
- md5: EBOOT bcfe1be4e67c5adf761dcc6e8da59e36, ovl_w00a e178c39f68e9d41ca36b3a1b6c9bacbf, ovl_w01a 7fc2f8a82ff3c7489f5d1a21a46e8bef (checked on the PC). SLES_503.83, mgs2disc.pak, pad.txt, memcard0 copied from MGS2T_v11.
- **HW run 2026-10-20 (`prof/hw_v12/`):** 1200 w01a steps mean **36.29 ms = logic 18.00 + draw pipeline 15.11 + IOP 1.93 + GS walk 1.26** (v10 41.62 = 21.11 + 17.26 + 1.99 + 1.27); window vb 24001-24100 logic 18.26 + IOP 1.96 = 20.22 against the model's 18.71 (the PspProf window pauses only in the draw pipeline and the GS walk, so it includes IOP + sound): model 7.5% low here (v10: 3.6% low), inferred from the VFPU ops' latencies the model does not charge; the HW game state had left the reference by then, so the work is similar but not identical. Hashes: ee and iop equal to `prof/hashes_x86_w01a_vfpu_vfb.txt` to vb 15500, ee differs from vb 16000 (iop equal throughout); the vfb count equals the x86 one to vb 5500 (3,239), then is lower (vb 6000: 30,835 against 48,209; end 289,956 against 465,732). Inferred: the group check `vcmp ES; nop; mfvc $131` reads the condition code before vcmp has written it, so failed checks pass (PPSSPP has no such delay). Fixed in v14 (bvtl), measured by hwprobe.c probe 7. Probe results: VFPU = FPU round-to-nearest 4096/4096 (add, sub, mul), vdot.q neither; denormal inputs and results flushed to signed zero; VFPU NaN = 0x7f800001; vmax / vmin of +0 / -0 give -0 in every lane (both orders); vcmp ES flags NaN, +-Inf, 0xfffc56e0, not max / denormal / -0; per macro op VFPU in registers + one check 45.5 ns, in memory 62.1 ns, FPU C form 387.8 ns; I-miss 218 ns per line (about 73 cycles).

## Build v15 (2026-10-20 14:08, delivered to Desktop\mg2 port\MGS2T_v15)
- v14 + `tools/ldu_fuse.py` (LDL / LDR and SDL / SDR pairs as one unaligned access) + `tools/jalr_tab.py` (JALR target cache) + a fresh PGO profile (ELF on deck + w01a, w00a on deck, w01a on w01a, all under `PPSSPP_VFPU_RN=1`; `RTXX=-Wno-error=coverage-mismatch` for boot_rt) + layouts from **full-step** ITRACEs (10 steps each: deck order `prof/layout_order_deck_v15.txt` on ELF + w00a, simulated I-misses 1,011 k -> 653 k; w01a overlay `prof/layout_order_w01a_v15.txt` with `-fix0`, 543 k -> 443 k) + boot.c split finder + hwprobe.c probes 8-10. Build dir /home/claude/v15/use (ephemeral); EBOOT repacked with the title "MGS2 timing harness v15" (same ELF payload).
- **Split finder** (boot.c end, x86 and PSP): `MGS2_HASH_DENSE=15700:16010` hashes every vblank of that range and prints "vuh: vb N <FNV32 of VU0 state>"; `MGS2_HASH_REF=hashref.txt` (lines "vb ee vfb [vuh]") compares every hash taken; the first ee / vfb difference prints "split: first difference at vb ..." + VF / ACC / Q / I / CLIP / MAC / VI and writes EE RAM to `split.<vb>` (prefix `MGS2_HASH_REF_DUMP`). hashref.txt = the x86 RN reference (dense 15700-16010 with vuh, every 100 from `prof/hashes_x86_w01a_vfpu_vfb.txt`), 559 lines.
- Container PPSSPP (`PPSSPP_VFPU_RN=1`): w01a all hashes taken equal to the reference (53 compared, incl. vb 16000 vfb 329,622 and vb 25000), no "split:" line; deck 48 / 50 compared equal (`prof/hashes_x86_deck_vfpu.txt`). PspProf full steps (`prof 24001 24101 all`, w01a, 100 steps): 3.95 M instructions, 44.4 k I-miss, 21.2 k D-miss per step = **30.4 ms (v14 35.6: 4.15 M, 65.3 k, 21.2 k; HW v14 full step 35.83)**.
- mgs2.cfg = v14's + `MGS2_HASH_DENSE=15700:16010` + `MGS2_HASH_REF=hashref.txt` (copy in `psp/v15/`). md5: EBOOT 0af764a93efcb7c8682709dd7ad5bc81, ovl_w00a 7578d04984d35200bd77a15437843461, ovl_w01a 3416e5cb4ad3f788160838233e7f4ae4, hashref.txt ff436da1b39e4daf1163ee06485a3812 (checked on the PC).
- **On the PSP (Ffm, 2026-10-20 14:44, `prof/hw_v15/`):** w01a 1200 steps mean **31.50 ms = logic 15.23 + draw 13.07 + IOP 1.93 + GS 1.27** (v14 35.83; model 30.4, 3.5% low). Game state equal to x86 at every hash to vb 15793, then **split at vb 15794** (ee 123923e4f246e4c5 against 03cc99be902127a5; vfb 325041 against 325091: the PSP fell back 50 times fewer in that vblank). Probe 7: the bvtl check reads the right condition 4096 / 4096 (v12's `vcmp; nop; mfvc` wrong 4095 / 4096). Probe 9: vmov.q bit-exact for every pattern; vmin / vmax order sign-magnitude bit patterns (NaN patterns included), keep denormals. Probe 8: overflow gives Inf, vcmp ES sees it; the arctangent group on +max falls back on the PSP too.
- **Cause of the split (x86, 2026-10-20):** in 0x11cf88 (the arctangent caller) lane z reads x = +0 and y = 0x0031685c, a garbage denormal at 0x1b47c8. The PSP FPU returns 0 + d = d (TT lessons A9: x +- 0 unchanged, even a denormal); x86 with FTZ gave 0, so FDIV saw a zero divisor (-max) and the arctangent run (0x11d02c) overflowed and fell back, while on the PSP lane z was -1 and the run stayed on the VFPU, rounding its other lanes to nearest (74 words of RAM 1-3 ulp apart). An x86 build with that FPU rule modelled gives the PSP's hashes exactly at vb 15794, 15795 and 15796. Fixes in v16: guest.h FADD / FSUB (tools/fpu_zero.py), and the fallbacks of VFPU runs and VFPU forms run with the VFPU's rounding (vu0.h VU_FB_ON, vupipe.py VPF) so a garbage lane can no longer change the other lanes' bits; the fallbacks' result clamps became VU_FCL (tools/vgrp_fcl.py), since the PSP's identity clamp only holds under round toward zero. SLES_503.83, mgs2disc.pak, pad.txt, memcard0 copied from MGS2T_v14.

- **What it measures:** the zero-time clock (speed_notes §32, Ffm's "A"; first PSP run with it, so every hash is new) and the reduced renderer on undisplayed steps (§33: OPT_LITE 64, gen.o built with `-DGEN_LITE` in genf.txt, 0x1214a0 returns at entry on a reduced call).
- **Build z5** (/home/claude/v20/z5, ephemeral) = v19 p3 + ZT boot_rt.o / gen_dispatch.o (z1, §32) + the reduced-pipeline rule in boot_rt.o + GEN_LITE gen.o (z2) + main_psp.o; rebuilt with `W=/home/claude/v19/nat/WP TITLE="MGS2 timing harness v20" sh tools/dev/rebuild_use.sh <dir> <objects>`. The v18 profile and code order are kept, as in v19, on purpose (one change per run): the changed functions run without profile (layout files and gcda dated 2026-10-20 18:20-21:10); fresh PGO + order go with the next build.
- **main_psp.c:** HARNESS_VERSION v20; cfg 8 "reduced renderer off" (`~64u`, NCFG 9); prints both `speedups: MGS2_OPT=` and the cycle; `out()` buffer 256 -> 512 bytes (v20's longer version line cut the end of the folder line and merged the next line into it: seen in the emulator with z3).
- **boot.c:** `g_opt = ~OPT_LITE` (was ~0u): the reduced renderer is off unless mgs2.cfg turns it on, since from boot it is not exact yet. Code byte-identical to z4 (objdump), only that .data word differs; the ELF's symbols and all 8 overlays are identical. x86 builds made before this change (x86lt) still default to lite on: pass `MGS2_OPT=ffffffbf` there.
- **Deck route cfg:** `MGS2_OPT=ffffffbf`, `MGS2_OPT_CYCLE=24000:1:ffffffff,ffffffbf` (odd vblanks: reduced calls, cfg 0 "all speedups on"; even vblanks: full calls, cfg 8 "reduced renderer off"), `MGS2_VFPU_STAT=1`, `MGS2_HASH_DENSE=15700:16010`, `MGS2_HASH_REF=hashref.txt` = `prof/hashref_x86_deck_v20.txt`. x86 hashes: `prof/hashes_x86_deck_v20.txt` (run VC2 with x86lt and this cfg; equal to `hashes_x86_deck_zt.txt` to vb 24000; after it ee differs from the full-renderer run because renderer memory does, gs / iop / vfb do not).
- **Reading the times:** the GS walk at vblank v walks call v - 1's packets, so with configs alternating every vblank each per-config line in log.txt carries the other config's walk. `tools/dev/steps_percall.py steps.csv` gives draw, the walk of the call's own packets and the step with its own walk per config.
- **Emulator (`prof/emu_v20/`):** z5 with the delivered folder: ee / iop / vfb 53 / 53 against `prof/hashes_x86_deck_v20.txt`, split finder 360 / 360 ee and vfb, vuh 311 / 311, no split. Same cfg without the MGS2_OPT line (`log_without_opt_line.txt`, checks the new default): every hash and vuh line identical. z3 and z4 gave the same hashes. PPSSPP's clock (not the PSP's; it counts instructions, not cache misses): reduced call draw 17.83 + its walk 4.94, full call 20.74 + 7.61; step with its own walk 40.81 against 46.29 ms.
- **md5 (checked on the PC):** EBOOT a57a04cd8f2c951351b7c554d61d7a5d, ovl_w00a f92e84c56ce6dedb3f38f5cd0586239c, ovl_w01a b5dee959f19b35fd4ae4b0965643e085, mgs2.cfg 5e864de7ef38da1023f8e2f1200ead5d, hashref 0dd345ff89d894abbdd0a15c03f18c07, README 8491b3d250447ba20480edc1768dff0b. ## Build v21 (2026-10-21 17:34, delivered to Desktop\mg2 port\MGS2T_v21)
- **What it measures:** the reduced renderer on from boot (the build's default, `g_opt = ~0u`; checked from boot on the PC, speed_notes §34) with a fresh PGO profile and code order (v19 and v20 reused v18's on changed code), plus the research_review.md harness items: M13 log saved every window, M18 `scePowerTick(PSP_POWER_TICK_ALL)` every window, the loaded modules listed at start (sceKernelGetModuleIdList + QueryModuleInfo), steps.csv columns `full` / `lite` (calls of each kind per vblank; `tools/dev/steps_percall.py` reads them, M16). M17 (the 1.4 s step) is diagnosed in speed_notes §35; its fix goes in v22.
- **Lite rule (boot_rt.o):** reduced iff OPT_LITE && k_vblanks != dg_shown_vb && k_vblanks - dg_shown_vb < 2, so every call in a shown vblank is full (v20 reduced the second call of a two-call vblank).
- **Builds** (ephemeral, /home/claude/v21): b = v20 z5 + that boot_rt.o + main_psp.o v21; c = PGO=use rebuild of b (5 objects) from runs ga (ELF profile, deck then w01a), gb (w00a overlay, deck), gc (w01a overlay, w01a), `tools/dev/pgo_run.sh` cfg "pgo 24000 25150"; d = c relinked with the new order (`tools/dev/relink_order.sh <order> d`, new; W=/home/claude/v19/nat/WP, VP vp16n, TITLE "MGS2 timing harness v21"). genf.txt as v20 (`-DGUEST_SPRSTATE -DGEN_LITE`).
- **Log fix (found in the PGO runs, speed_notes §35):** the first v21 main_psp.o appended log.txt with `freopen(..., "a", stderr)` each window; pspsdk's libcglue never closes a file reopened on fds 0-2, so each window leaked one of the 64 I/O handles and every log stopped at vb 5900. Now stderr is a `funopen` stream into a 128 KB buffer appended with sceIoOpen / sceIoWrite / sceIoClose each window (main_psp.c log_put / log_write / log_commit); failed writes are counted and reported in the summary. The PGO runs were redone after the fix (b, ga, gb, gc relinked).
- **PGO:** all four runs equal the PC (ee / iop 50 / 50 against `prof/hashes_x86_{deck,w01a}_v21.txt`); a warnings-on recompile of c's five objects (`-S -o <name>.s` in the build dir, so gcc finds the gcda through auxname; `-o /dev/null` does not) shows no profile mismatch.
- **Code order:** `trace_run.sh` IT=1 on c, 8 busy windows per route (prof 24001 24006 ... 25051 25056) -> layout_funcs.py -> `layout_sim -and` over both routes, -wl 2 -wd 1 -test 2, two seeds x 40,000 iterations; seed 1 kept: weighted I-misses 5,787,518 -> 4,095,165 (-29.2%) on the fitted windows, 1,742,814 -> 1,278,523 (-26.6%) on the held-out ones. `layout_sim -new` on d equals the search exactly (deck 2,693,523 / held out 818,203; w01a 1,401,642 / 460,320); both routes give the same ELF order. ## Build v24 (2026-10-23 12:40, delivered to Desktop\mg2 port\MGS2T_v24 and MGS2T_v24w)
- **What it measures:** the facts the 2026-10-23 audit asked for (`audit_2026-10-23.md`). The game code is v23's (gen.o, gen_dispatch.o, gen_s_w00a.o, gen_s_w01a.o and iop_main.o byte-identical), so the hashes are v21's. (1) The log's first line is the build's own title: psp/Makefile passes PSP_EBOOT_TITLE to main_psp.c as HARNESS_TITLE (audit L8; v23's banner still said v22). (2) Memory (M2): `mem_report()` in psp/main_psp.c prints newlib's mallinfo (the arena taken from the heap block, in use, free and in how many chunks, top), the heap block's start (`__psp_heap_blockid`) and the program break, at the start and at the end; at the end it also takes every block malloc can still give, largest first (each held until the next is found, then all freed). (3) Disc reads (M3): runtime/iop_host.h counts the pack reads (`g_disc_reads`, `g_disc_sectors`, and `g_disc_us` on the harness's clock, which only the PSP harness sets); steps.csv has three new columns (disc_n, disc_kb, disc_us), the log a "disc vb" line for every 100-vblank window with reads and a total at the end. (4) Hashes (M5): boot.c's state_hash takes `MGS2_HASH_EVERY=<n>[,<vb>:<m>]`; `500,24000:100` adds a hash every 100 vblanks from 24000 (13 on the timed deck steps, the last at the stop; v23: 3). (5) No MGS2_OPT_CYCLE: every speedup is on in every step (v23 took turns between three settings). (6) The crew deck (w01a; not run on the PSP since v18, audit H3) as a second folder, MGS2T_v24w, with the same ELF and overlays: its EBOOT (EBOOT_w.PBP) differs from the deck's only in PARAM.SFO (title "MGS2 timing harness v24 crew deck"; DATA.PSP identical), and its cfg adds v18's MGS2_POKE line, so w01a loads at vb 23337.

Memory as the crew deck run. **Disc reads (M3):** the 12 read steps spend 12.9-19.4 ms reading (2 reads of 36-58 KB) and 31.0 ms at vb 25000 (4 reads, 128 KB); their IOP+sound column is 20.9-51.2 ms against 2.58 ms mean (2.55 median, 3.38 max) in the 1,188 steps without reads, so besides the read itself those steps spend 5-8 ms more on the IOP side (20 ms for the 128 KB step: about 0.15 ms per KB read) [I: the IOP code handling the streamed data]. Whole read steps 61.6-112.6 ms. Disc pack over the run: 2,513 reads, 74,956 KB, 17.68 s (7.0 ms per read).

## Build v25f (2026-10-23, delivered 18:17 to Desktop\mg2 port\MGS2T_v25f after the v24s log was read; md5s checked on the PC)
- **What it measures:** free play on the deck (audit H1). Every deck timing before was Otacon's scripted tutorial (speed_notes §41-42). The pad (`play/pad_v25f.txt` = pad_free + the mix from `play/mix.py`) plays v24's opening, taps START at vb 23,410 and every 1,000 vblanks from 24,000 to 29,000 (skips the tutorial and d01t's boarding scene), and from vb 30,500 plays 2 minutes on the aft deck: stand 10 s, walk 36 s, crawl 8 s, punch and fire 8 s, first person 12 s, then into the guards' view (an alert) 46 s. Timed steps vb 30,500-36,500 (`deck 30500`). **Correction 2026-10-25: no alert happens in that last part** (speed_notes §56).
- **Program:** v24's code at v24's addresses (every v24 object linked as it is; main_psp.o rebuilt only for the title, at the same length) plus free play's extra functions E = (cov_prefork + cov_mix) - cov_u (v24's coverage): ELF 46, w00a 516, d01t 509 (1,071). `tools/xunit.py` copies each E body from the full translation under an X name (calls between E functions direct, every other call to the reduced unit's function or stub); the ELF's go into gen_x.o, a stage's into an extra part after the overlay's own end (`psp/ovl_x.ld`; the reduced part keeps its bytes: cmp against the plain link). v24's stubs reach them through `--wrap=rt_missing` (`psp/xhook.c` x_call: a 512-entry direct-mapped cache, then the ELF's table, then the loaded stage's; a stage's translation holds a few functions at ELF addresses that the ELF's lacks, w00a 106020 / 112fb8 / 155370 and d01t 108520 / 118b30 / 11eb78 / 12a7c0, so an ELF address also looks in the stage's table: emulator run e1 stopped at vb 29,068 on 0x106020 without that). `--wrap=mgs2_main / mgs2_exit` start the battery thread and print the hook's counts at the end; `--defsym xh_k_vblanks` = boot.c's static k_vblanks.
- **battery.txt (new):** a thread (priority 0x11) writes a line at the start, every 60 s, on any change of external power / charging / low battery, and at the end: seconds since the start, the last vblank the hook saw (-1 before its first call at vb 23,400), level %, mV, temperature, minutes left, charging status, external power, charging, low. Each line is opened, appended and closed, so it survives a power-off. Reads only (scePower).
- **Layout (cache sets kept):** v24's .text unchanged; the libraries' text +0x1bf98, the import stubs +0x1bfd8; .rodata +0x1c000 / +0x1e000; .data +0x1e000 (v24's objects) / +0x20000 (the libraries'); .sdata, .sbss, .bss +0x20000; ovl_arena 0x08bec000 (v24 0x08bcc000). `psp/v25f_pad.S` fills PAD_TEXT 0x17c8, PAD_RODATA 0x1c80, PAD_DATA 0xa0. `tools/dev/elfcmp.py` against v24: every v24 symbol at its address (code) or moved by a multiple of 8 KB (data); every plain overlay the size of v24's.
- **Build:** `psp/v25f.sh` (container inputs: v24/d, the full translation h1size/full_l and play/st/d01t_l, the coverage lists in play/run, vglz/W/cov_u.txt), build dir /home/claude/v25f/rb2 (ephemeral). Disc pack: `tools/make_pak.py` over the route's table reads (`play/disc_reads_v25f.log`, 3,082 lines): mgs2disc.pak 85,299,200 bytes, md5 0792607195dcb838626456a41c8278e3, made again on the PC with the same tool.
- **x86 checks:** reference for this route with the reduced renderer on: `prof/hashes_x86_free_v25f.txt`, `prof/hashref_x86_free_v25f.txt` (the delivered hashref.txt, 672 lines). M1 (speed_notes §40): the PS2 FPU rule build B equals A in ee / iop / vuh and in every VIF1 + GIF packet to vb 36,500 (`prof/m1_denormal_v25f_free.txt`). The reduced renderer keeps the game's path on this route: 69 vblanks with different read values, none with a different read count or address; the values are leftover lanes two copy routines read (`prof/rh_lite_free_v25f.txt`, `prof/rl_lite_free_v25f.txt`; picture check of those vblanks still open).
- **Emulator (PPSSPP JIT, the delivered files, run e2; `prof/emu_v25f/`):** every state hash equal to the PC (124 / 124 every 100 vblanks, 431 / 431 against hashref, vuh 311 / 311, no split); hook 1,038,884 calls (ELF 10,451, overlays 1,028,433), 0 not found, 95.6 per timed step; no VU0 interpreter call after vb 29,068; no disc read in the timed steps. - **Model (PspProf, `prof/model_v25f_free.txt`):** walk 85.36, crawl 87.66, first person 82.24, alert 87.10 ms per step (tutorial 72.40): about 82.8 ms on the PSP if the model is 3.4% high as on v24's deck [I]. Instructions +6.5%, I-misses +90%. A third of the extra misses is the call path into the stage (4.33 ms per step against 0.78): S_w00a_call's switch, laid out by the tutorial's profile, walks chains of hundreds of compares for targets the profile never saw.
- **Cfg:** v24's with `vblanks 36500`, `deck 30500` and `MGS2_HASH_EVERY=500,30500:100` (61 checks in the timed steps). **md5 (container):** EBOOT 1ea7184c948cfc1649879249de97a2e8, ovl_w00a de9985c610498ffba06786d313e01978, ovl_d01t 93b520b72b52ba63cab8d797fc1a8759, ovl_w01a 8a54f0acbcfacb590d99ff8b5e47bdd7, ovl_d00t e8feed638474381aabc3103c545266da, ovl_init c2d64009b311d5438512a554adfa4988, ovl_n_title 356cbf1769a86707a69f0b0a16fe0094, ovl_r_title 6d1f27ae23644c300acec370293f29d7, ovl_r_tnk0 382fc1c19299792c16443118a6cca924, mgs2.cfg b6f49e6da0eadc93ba7d40c787cab461, hashref c53ebd00bc1e4031828f7ff30acc5523, pad.txt f80619bd58e1e66862a8bb74ea8844a6, README 8384cef90f6573d0d2d5a4bc9f8df56f.

## Build v26 (2026-10-23, delivered 21:05 to Desktop\mg2 port\MGS2T_v26 after the v25f log was read; md5s checked on the PC; Ffm asked to run it 21:16)
- **What it measures:** v25f's free play (the same pad.txt, hashref.txt and disc pack; timed steps vb 30,501-36,500) on v26's code, so the times compare step for step with v25f's 92.42 ms (speed_notes §44-45).
- **Program (exact; speed_notes §45):** (1) stage calls by table: `tools/stage_tab.py` replaces each stage's S_<stage>_call switch by a sorted table of the same cases and a 256-entry direct-mapped cache in the overlay's bss (zeroed when it loads); `OPT_STAGE_TAB` (512, on by default; `runtime/host_time.h`) lets an address from 0x1f1c00 up skip gen_call's search (`boot/dispatch.h` guest_call) and the overlay's name compare (`psp/main_psp.c`). (2) Free play's 1,071 functions are compiled in their own units (v25f's W25 headers for gen_elf.h, w00a and d01t; the other stages vglz's), with no hook. (3) PGO profiles from three routes (deck tutorial, crew deck, free play; libgcov merges them) and the code order from instruction traces of all three. `psp/main_psp.c` as it was for v26 is kept in `psp/v26/main_psp.c`.
- **Build:** `psp/v26.sh` steps hdr, ga, pga, gb, gc, pgbc, c, trace, order, d, emu (build dir /home/claude/v26, ephemeral). Order: layout_sim over one w00a trace (deck windows 1-7, free 1-7, then deck 8 and free 8 held out) and the crew deck's, two seeds x 40,000: seed 1 kept. Simulated I-misses against c (the new profiles with v24's order): deck + free 6,678,382 -> 4,176,371 (-37%), held out 945,409 -> 599,422 (-37%); crew deck 1,136,308 -> 815,502 (-28%), held out 367,009 -> 261,348 (-29%) (seed 2: 4,286,524 / 842,568). `layout_sim -new` on d gives the search's own counts. Text 3,985,076 bytes (v25f 4,061,872); ovl_arena 0x08bd8000.
- **x86 checks** (/home/claude/v26/x86, the v26 headers, OPT_STAGE_TAB on): the deck, crew deck and free routes' hash files are byte-identical to `prof/hashes_x86_{deck,w01a}_v21.txt`, `prof/hashes_x86_free_v25f.txt` and their hashref files.
- **Emulator (PPSSPP JIT, PPSSPP_VFPU_RN=1, the delivered EBOOT and overlays; `prof/emu_v26/`):** deck and crew deck (v24's cfgs): ee / iop / vfb 63 / 63 against x86, 370 / 370 against hashref, vuh 311 / 311, no split; free (the delivery's cfg): 124 / 124 against x86, 431 / 431 against hashref, vuh 311 / 311, no split. No call through a register left unresolved. PPSSPP's clock: deck 36.57 ms per step (v24 36.54), crew deck 12.22 (12.21), free 43.80 (v25f 44.20). Memory in PPSSPP at the end: malloc can still give 9,632 KB (v25f 9,552).

The call path into the stage 4.33 -> 1.01 ms per step; the rest of the code -0.71 ms in the walk window (the new order cut I-misses by 32% against build c, the same program and profiles with v24's order, which itself missed more than v25f). PSP with v25f's ratios for the same steps: 86.9 / 89.2 / 85.0 / 89.4 ms, the whole timed run about 89.0 [I] (told to Ffm as "near 88"). The profiled windows (PPSSPP's interpreter) are not exact from vb 32,000 on, in v25f's model too: the walk window is the clean comparison.

## Build v26me (2026-10-23, built 21:30; held on the PC in _claude_tmp\held\MGS2T_v26me (21:42) until v26's log was read; described to Ffm and moved to Desktop\mg2 port\MGS2T_v26me 2026-10-24 11:18, md5s checked there)
- **What it measures:** the game's voice mix on the Media Engine (Ffm's card, 18:28; sound_notes.md "The voice mix split"): what the full mix costs the ME (me_steps.csv: ME busy time, commands and renders per step from vb 30,500; "mix vb" lines every 100 vblanks), what the split costs the main CPU (engine A, the commands, the waits for a full ring or a barrier) and whether the main CPU's game work slows while the ME works (the shared bus): steps.csv step by step against v26's. Nothing is played; B's samples go into a CRC, compared with the PC every 10 s of sound ("mix: ck" lines against `prof/mix_ck_x86_free_v26me.txt`, 73 on this route).
- **Program:** v26's objects at v26's addresses (`psp/v26me.sh`; `tools/dev/elfcmp.py`: every v26 code symbol at its address, data moved by multiples of 8 KB). New: main_psp.o (`psp/v26/main_psp.c`, only the title differs, at the same length; with v26's title it gives v26's object), the split (`boot/spu_split.c`, PS2RC's `audio/spu2_split.c`), the PSP side (`psp/me_mix.c`), the ME's loop (`psp/me_mix_me.c`, also built with -DME_SIM for `MGS2_SPU_ME=2`), PS2RC's bring-up (`hle/psp/hle_me.c`, `ps2rc_kcall.S`, unchanged), the battery log (`psp/v26me_bat.c`) and `psp/v26me_pad.S`, linked after v26's objects with seven --wrap (spu2_bind_ops, spu2_render48, iop_rt_create, host_vb_begin, mgs2_exit, sceKernelExitGame, mgs2_main). Pads (`tools/dev/padfit.py`, 4 refits) 0x310 / 0x1464 / 0x1e40 / 0x1c94. The libraries' text is not at v26's cache offsets (270 inputs moved by 0xe0b4 / 0xdef0 / 0xdd44, mod 8 KB 0xb4 / 0x1ef0 / 0x1d44: the new units pull in more of newlib): measured in the model below. ovl_arena 0x08bea000 (v26 0x08bd8000, +9 x 8 KB); the overlays are v26's objects relinked there, each the size of v26's.
- **The ME:** ARK-4's KUBridge (kuKernelCall) runs hle_me.c's start in kernel mode: the reset stub copied to the ME's reset vector, the ME's reset released; the stub sets the ME's memory range and bus clock enables (no clock speed), its exception handler (records and halts) and jumps to me_mix_work. scePowerLock held while the ME runs; the ME back in reset at the end and before a HOME exit. A 2 s stall or an ME exception stops the split (log.txt says why) and the game goes on as v26. Ring 16,384 commands x 8 B (128 KB), checkpoint slots 1,024, the ME's own region 64 KB (32 KB stack). Without the `MGS2_SPU_ME` line the program runs as v26 (the split off).
- **battery.txt:** v25f's lines (start, every 60 s, on a change of external power / charging / low, HOME, end) plus the ME's state: off / on / simulated / stopped, heartbeat, state, commands pushed and consumed, checkpoints. Each line opened, appended and closed. After the end it goes on a line a minute until exit.
- **Emulator (PPSSPP JIT, PPSSPP_VFPU_RN=1, the delivered files, v25f's free route; container /home/claude/v26me/emu):** `MGS2_SPU_ME` unset, =1 (PPSSPP: "mix: off (Media Engine not started: emulator ...)") and =2 (the ME's loop run by the main CPU whenever it would wait): each 124 / 124 hashes against x86 and its 431 hash lines identical to v26's emulator log (`prof/emu_v26/log_free.txt`), no split. =2: checkpoints 73 / 73 equal to the PC (frames and commands, CRC, B's state hash) and 73 "eq" (B = A); 592,021 commands (581,407 writes dropped as no-ops), 292,000 renders, 35,040,000 frames, 3,905 SPU RAM writes (11,211 KB) each behind a barrier. PPSSPP gives the battery voltage as an error code (-2147352262).
- **Cfg:** v26's plus `MGS2_SPU_ME=1` and `MGS2_ME_STEPS_FROM=30500`; hashref.txt = `prof/hashref_x86_free_v25f.txt`.
- **Model (PspProf, the walk window vb 31,501-31,600, `MGS2_SPU_ME` unset: v26me's layout without the split; container /home/claude/v26me/model):** 81.13 ms per step against v26's 81.34 (12.055 / 12.082 M instructions, 75.2 / 75.4 k I-misses, 69.7 / 69.9 k D-misses); the libraries 1.71 against 1.70 ms, their I-misses 0.8 k in both. So the moved library text costs nothing measurable; the small differences elsewhere are of the interpreted window's known inexactness (speed_notes §45: instruction counts in the game's code differ by 0.2% though vb 31,500 is equal). Reading the PSP run: a step-time difference from v26 beyond a few tenths of a ms is the split's main-CPU work or the Media Engine on the bus.
- **md5 (checked on the PC):** EBOOT 94818e7c15e05b70f1a9bfebf0406f54, ovl_w00a 38265b84726958d16738e17aad4f4b86, ovl_d01t 73b56c1a87d212cbdf992d170c15dc32, ovl_w01a 6a1399782ecdfa3062300d3332b65971, ovl_d00t 09306a734247b0412df4f3c0ccd44c27, ovl_init 24c547e25021d4b63f1e1cc1c2f64c2d, ovl_n_title a80701f1ffa3f0e2459c0b14f8da0d24, ovl_r_title 274f1f5a991dc11e0313a0611581e4b5, ovl_r_tnk0 65cf99aca7de865660a42c3de9a7656e, mgs2.cfg 4345347dc57db834e6e07b2e40fd8fad, hashref c53ebd00bc1e4031828f7ff30acc5523, README 657749d77a8dd63bc9929c7efaa2b904; pad.txt (f80619bd58e1e66862a8bb74ea8844a6), SLES_503.83, mgs2disc.pak (0792607195dcb838626456a41c8278e3) and memcard0 copied from MGS2T_v26 on the PC.
- **PSP run (Ffm, 2026-10-24 12:10; `prof/hw_v26me/` log.txt, steps.csv, battery.txt; no me_steps.csv):** to the end (stop at vb 36,500). The Media Engine did not start: "mix: off (Media Engine not started: heartbeat DEAD (boots 0->0, state 0, heartbeat 0->0, reset reg was 00000040); power callback 0)", so the run is v26 with the split off. All 431 hash lines and 311 vuh lines identical to v26's PSP log, no split. Free play 88.76 ms/step (v26 88.72): logic 47.72 / draw 33.60 / IOP 2.53 / GS 4.91; `tools/dev/steps_parts.py` +0.02 to +0.05 ms per part. 8 steps +7 ms (vb 30,752 ... 35,752): one step after each battery line's vblank, the battery log's file write on the game thread, once a minute. Cause [I] and fix: sound_notes.md "The voice mix split on the PSP", Build v26me2.

## Build v26me2 (2026-10-24, built 12:31; v26me's log read first; described to Ffm 12:27; delivered 12:40 to Desktop\mg2 port\MGS2T_v26me2, md5s checked on the PC)
- **One change against v26me:** the bring-up is `psp/me/hle_me_mgs2.c`, the port's copy of PS2RC's hle_me.c (PS2RC's file stays unchanged). Its reset stub writes the ME's 0xBC100040 = 2, 0xBC100050 = 6 and the NMI flags first (PS2RC's values; Sony's and mcidclan's order), keeps the values it found, sets Status and its exception handler, and only then reads the boot block (which lies above the 32 MB line, 0x0B14D940). Progress words at 0xBFC00400, in the ME's boot memory next to the stub (Sony's ME program uses 0x600-0x71F): [0] stage 1 registers written / 2 main RAM read and written / 3 caches done, jumping; [1] [2] the ME's 0x40 / 0x50 as found; [3] the boot block's check word as the ME read it (0x4D473245); [4] the control block's first word read through main RAM (HLE_ME_MAGIC 0x5A4D4531); [5] the loop's mark 0x4C4F4F50 (`psp/me_mix_me.c`, -DME_PROG_MARK); [8..11] the exception record (Cause, EPC, BadVAddr, 0xDEADBEEF; a second exception keeps the first one's). k_start clears them and reads the main CPU's own 0xBC100040 (a read); k_marks reads them back in kernel mode after the start, on both outcomes; log.txt "me:" line (`psp/me_mix.c`, -DHLE_ME_PROGRESS). A user-mode sceKernelDcacheWritebackInvalidateAll before the kernel call. The OR register variant is gone (it needed the boot block first).
- **Program:** `psp/v26me2.sh` (v26me.sh with those units, PADS="0x128 0x1364 0x1de0 0x1c54"): `tools/dev/elfcmp.py` against v26me: every symbol v26me had at v26me's address (3,507 text symbols, the import stubs, rodata, data, bss) except the changed units' own; the overlays byte-identical to v26me's (arena 0x08bea000). Build folder /home/claude/v26me2/c (ephemeral).
- **Emulator (PPSSPP JIT, PPSSPP_VFPU_RN=1, v25f's free route; /home/claude/v26me/emu tags m2me, s2me):** `MGS2_SPU_ME=1`: "me: stage 0 of 3 ..." (the emulator refuses before the kernel call) and "mix: off (... emulator ...)"; =2 (simulated): 73 / 73 checkpoints eq, identical to v26me's. Both: 431 hash lines identical to v26me's emulator logs (m26b, s26b), vuh identical, no split.
- **PC folder:** v26me's files copied on the PC (overlays, SLES_503.83, mgs2disc.pak, pad.txt, hashref.txt, memcard0) plus the new EBOOT.PBP and README.txt; mgs2.cfg = v26me's with a first comment line naming v26me2 (md5 1ea5e6603dc6ca4cdf5565af01b03616).
- **md5:** EBOOT 2b5eca887324552ba0cbeb500b37a2be; ovl_*.bin = v26me's.
- **PSP run (Ffm, 2026-10-24 13:30; `prof/hw_v26me2/`: log_poweroff.txt, battery.txt; steps.csv and me_steps.csv are written only at the end):** the Media Engine started: "mix: on (Media Engine started: heartbeat 1433, Count 0.0 MHz, scePowerLock 0, power callback 0, ME registers found 00000001 / 00000000, reset register 00000040)". It mixed the whole run: 67 / 67 checkpoints "eq" and equal to the PC's (`prof/mix_ck_x86_free_v26me.txt`: frames, commands, CRC, B's state hash); at every battery line the commands pushed = consumed. All 401 hash lines (to vb 33,500) and 311 vuh lines identical to v26's PSP log. **Main CPU:** free play (vb 30,001-33,600, 36 windows) 89.08 ms per step against v26's 88.97 on the same windows (+0.11: logic +0.05, IOP+sound +0.05); the tutorial's windows +0.18 (IOP+sound +0.15). Mixing on the main CPU cost +7.53 ms per deck step (v24s). **The ME's busy time is not measured:** its CP0 Count reads 0 on the PSP ("ME busy 0.00" in every "mix vb" line). Its loop polls an empty ring about every 75 us (heartbeat 802.7 k per minute at t 180-240 s, the intro with 8 commands per step); in free play 749 k per minute (t 2,220-2,280 s): 6.7% fewer, so about 7 ms of the ME's time per step [I].
- **The PSP switched itself off** between vb 33,600 and 33,700, t = 2,313-2,324 s (the log's last commit is the window line of vb 33,600; that vblank's "mix vb" and hash lines, written after the commit, are missing). battery.txt's last line, t 2,280 s, vb 33,304: 71%, 4,050 mV, 20 C, no external power; the ME on, heartbeat 29,690,429, 481,936 commands pushed and consumed, 66 checkpoints. Ffm (13:45): found it off, and it started fresh (a real power-off, not sleep); (13:47) the battery is good. The spot is the route's first R1 press (pad 0008 at vb 33,600: first-person view, speed_notes §47's "first person" part), which v26 and v26me passed. **PC check** (x86 split with a write log, /home/claude/mex, ephemeral): hashes 339 / 339 to vb 33,900 against `prof/hashes_x86_free_v25f.txt`, checkpoints 67 / 67; the sound commands of vb 33,600-33,700: 3,065 parameter writes, 1 key on, 2 key off, 800 renders, no SPU RAM write (the last at vb ~28,672; 3,905 in all, none past the end of SPU RAM: PS2RC's iop_rt_libs.c sd_dma_to_spu clamps each to the 2 MB). **The ME's memory:** B 9,960 bytes at the bottom of its 64 KB region, its deepest stack about 5 KB of 32 KB (-fstack-usage: render_block 4,528 bytes); every address it computes into SPU RAM is masked (spu2_lite.c RAM_MASK). **Cause unknown.** v24's deck run 1 also powered off once, without the ME (vb 18,900-19,000, on USB power). PS2RC's hle_me.h: a suspend with the ME taken over hangs the kernel, "a power-off seen on hardware"; here scePowerLock was held (0). Next: Build v26me3 (the last seconds in battery.txt).

## Build v26me3 (2026-10-24; build d 13:58 without a crash catcher, build e 14:52 with it after Ffm chose "Add it" (card 14:41); v26me2's log read first; for v26me2's power-off; build e described to Ffm 15:05 and delivered to Desktop\mg2 port\MGS2T_v26me3, all 15 md5s checked on the PC)
- **Diagnostics only, against v26me2:** `-DME_DIAG3` on `psp/me_mix.c`, `psp/me_mix_me.c` (both builds) and `psp/v26me_bat.c` (`psp/v26me3.sh`). battery.txt gets a line every 5 s (v26me2: 60 s), each with the Media Engine's idle polls, the last command it applied (its two words), the EPC of an exception it took (0 = none) and the power callback's counts (power switch, parks) and last flags, so the last line shows the state at most 5 s before a stop. The "mix vb" lines add the ME's busy time from its idle polls (its Count register reads 0 on the PSP): polls per second with an empty ring are measured in the 100 ms after its start, before the split records anything; busy = the share of the window it did not poll. Without `-DME_DIAG3` the sources give v26me2's objects byte for byte (me_mix.o, me_mix_me.o, me_sim.o, v26me_bat.o). Dropped from the plan: a range check in the ME on each memory range it is handed (the ranges are bounded where they are made: PS2RC's iop_rt_libs.c clamps SPU DMA to the 2 MB, spu2_lite.c masks every address).
- **Crash catcher (build e; asked first on card 14:25 since it is a kernel-level change on Ffm's PSP; Ffm "Add it" 14:41; "can it brick?" answered 14:42: no, RAM only, nothing to flash, no power or clock setting).** Why: on this PSP a crash switched the console off (tt_port lessons A10), so v26me2's power-off may have been a main-CPU crash. Sources `psp/v26me_exc.c` + `psp/v26me_exc.S`, hooks in me_mix.c / v26me_bat.c under `-DME_EXC` (v26me3.sh `EXC=1`, which also links `-lpspsystemctrl_user`). Registered in kernel mode as the default exception handler: `sctrlHENFindFunction("sceExceptionManager", "ExceptionManagerForKernel", NID)` (register 0x0A998599, release 0x409DBB53 on 6.60; 0x565C0B0E / 0x291FF031 on 1.50, as tt_port's psp_game / psp_bench) called through PS2RC's `ps2rc_kcall` (KUBridge kuKernelCall) with handler | 0x80000000. The handler (asm) saves the registers to `mgs2_exc_regs` (r[32], status, lo, hi, badvaddr, cause, epc) and erets into `mgs2_exc_trap` on its own 16 KB stack; a later exception parks its thread (sceKernelSleepThread, 1 KB stack) and keeps the first record. `mgs2_exc_trap`: a syscall-free screen record first; with interrupts off at the exception it spins with "hold the power switch"; otherwise the full report (thread name and stack via sceKernelReferThreadStatus, code at EPC-16, stack words, EBOOT and arena ranges, the ME's state) on the screen (black, red text), in crash.txt and in log.txt (the harness's unwritten g_logbuf lines first, reached at fixed offsets from g_emuprof: LOG_DN 0x14a0, LOG_DBUF 0x14a4, LOG_DPATH 0x214a4, which v26me3.sh checks in the linked ELF), a battery.txt line (unless the crash is in the battery thread), then me_mix_halt (ME back in reset); then it waits for X, sceKernelVolatileMemUnlock(0), sceKernelExitGame (me_mix.c's wrap gives the handler back first, A10's order). HOME while it waits: main_psp.c's exit_cb gets no answer from the run within 2 s and calls sceKernelExitGame (the same wrap). Self-test at install with `MGS2_EXC=1`: thread exc_test (prio 0x30) does a misaligned `lw`; caught = recorded as the self-test, re-armed, the thread ends; not caught within 2 s = the thread is terminated, the handler given back and the run goes on without it. cfg `MGS2_EXC`: 1 on + self-test (the delivered cfg), 2 on, 0 or absent off. On PPSSPP (sceIoDevctl "kemulator:") the catcher stays off; `MGS2_EXC_DRY=<vb>` prints a made-up record at that vblank, which checks the report path only: registration, handler and self-test first run on the PSP.
- **Program (build e, folder /home/claude/v26me3/e, ephemeral):** pads 0x58f8 0x48b4 0x3dd8 0x266c (4 refits); `tools/dev/elfcmp.py` against v26: 3,017 text symbols +0 (v26's code at v26's addresses), newlib's and libgcc's code moved (modulo 8 KB: _Balloc +0xdc, __adddf3 +0x1cf4), data +0 modulo 8 KB; against v26me2: 3,046 +0, _Balloc +0x28, __adddf3 +0x1fb0 modulo 8 KB. ovl_arena 0x08bf8000 (v26 0x08bd8000, v26me2 0x08bea000), g_emuprof 0x09058000; mgs2_exc_trap 0x08b9179c, mgs2_exc_handler 0x08b91f58, mgs2_exc_park 0x08b92088, mgs2_exc_taken 0x0b15baf8, mgs2_exc_regs 0x0b15bafc, stack 0x0b15cce0-0x0b160ce0, park stack 0x0b160ce0. Build d (no catcher, folder d, never delivered): pads 0x3968 0x3254 0x1de0 0x1c54 (1 refit), ovl_arena 0x08bf0000, EBOOT md5 99f358ab4ee13e87543255c9c41f2ada. The first build (folder c) printed a made-up ME busy line in the emulator's simulation (idle rate 0); that line is now printed only for a real ME.
- **Emulator (PPSSPP, PPSSPP_VFPU_RN=1, v25f's free route; /home/claude/v26me3/emu; `prof/emu_v26me3/`):** build d, tags m3b / s3b (450 s each): both 431 / 431 hash lines and 311 / 311 vuh lines identical to v26's emulator log (`prof/emu_v26/log_free.txt`), no split; `MGS2_SPU_ME=1`: "mix: off (... emulator ...)"; =2 (simulated): 73 / 73 checkpoints eq and equal to the PC's (`prof/mix_ck_x86_free_v26me.txt`); battery.txt lines every 5 s with the new fields; no "ME idle polls" line in the simulation. Build e (run.sh now appends XCFG lines to mgs2.cfg and keeps crash.txt), tags e_me1 (`MGS2_EXC=1 MGS2_EXC_DRY=33650`, `MGS2_SPU_ME=1`, 415 s) and e_es2 (`MGS2_EXC=2 MGS2_EXC_DRY=30000`, `MGS2_SPU_ME=2`, 425 s): both 431 / 431 hash lines and 311 / 311 vuh = v26's, no split; e_es2 73 / 73 checkpoints = PC; crash.txt "crash catcher: off (emulator)" plus the dry-run record with the right thread ("user_main", stack 0bd9c000-0bfc0000), EBOOT code 08804000-08becfd0, overlay arena 08bf8000-09058000 and ME state. The code words at EPC read 0x68xxxxxx on PPSSPP, different per run: its JIT's block markers (0x68000000 | block number) in emulated RAM; the PSP shows the real code.
- **md5 (build e, delivered):** EBOOT ce0689294f006d07834d56bac464d929, ovl_d00t 109c38109de0a3bc712caef713f001c2, ovl_d01t 3aa18adfa7975e5fc42f1a3d95850e93, ovl_init 65192c6ef0d1e5754874eef99d74a3a8, ovl_n_title 76690dc0d7fa6d5f2ded8545ef5c21ec, ovl_r_title 12b4c34fb7870b27a60000bab00f617d, ovl_r_tnk0 6e888ef9943685229a0dc9010ab9d2ef, ovl_w00a 514034261ff07ebbe393ee2a17fed69b, ovl_w01a 9777b3e6adc3d02157b10c1481833d6f; mgs2.cfg 94760bf173c192f28d027721e39ff1d1 (v26me2's cfg verbatim, then `MGS2_EXC=1`), README.txt d59eea571756855e9072841dbca6906c. SLES_503.83, mgs2disc.pak, pad.txt and hashref.txt are v26me2's (e3f4c0df306b8567beddb9e899e83cd6, 0792607195dcb838626456a41c8278e3, f80619bd58e1e66862a8bb74ea8844a6, c53ebd00bc1e4031828f7ff30acc5523); memcard0 empty.
- **Asked of Ffm (15:05):** back up the memory stick; AC adapter if possible (a run-through then points at power); run to the end (~45 min); `MGS2_EXC=0` if it switches off during the self-test; photograph a crash screen; send log.txt, battery.txt, crash.txt, steps.csv, me_steps.csv.
- **Run 1 (Ffm 16:09, "crashed again"; log.txt, battery.txt, crash.txt):** stopped in the crash catcher's self-test, before the game started. log.txt ends with the start lines (`run: 36500 vblanks ...`; probes as in earlier runs, `MGS2_EXC=1` read), crash.txt holds only "crash catcher: on; self-test starting", battery.txt only its start line (t 0 s, 83%, 4068 mV, 20 C, external power 0): the PSP stopped within 5 s, during the deliberate misaligned load or in the handler / trap after it (no self-test record was written). So the default-exception-handler route (PSPSDK pspexception_asm.S convention, tt_port's psp_bench) does not work as built on Ffm's PSP (6.60 / ARK-4); cause not known [I]. Asked Ffm (16:15) whether it switched off or froze. Next run: `MGS2_EXC=0` (the PC copy's last line changed to `MGS2_EXC=0` with a comment; mgs2.cfg md5 now ebcdb9d02f85a2cdaa8bed127cfd4129); with 0 the catcher's code returns at once (the emulator check ran that path).
- **Ffm's answer (16:22): "PSP switch itself off"** during run 1's self-test (no freeze, no screen). So on Ffm's PSP a CPU exception (the self-test's deliberate misaligned `lw`) switches the console off, either through the system's default handler (ours never installed) or through a second exception in our handler / trap: the same symptom as v26me2's power-off, which makes a main-CPU or ME crash its likely cause [I]. The catcher is not used again until it is understood. **Run 2 started 16:24 with `MGS2_EXC=0`** (Ffm: "its running now", vb 600 at 16:24); asked for log.txt, battery.txt, steps.csv, me_steps.csv.
- **Run 2 (`MGS2_EXC=0`; Ffm 17:27, log.txt, battery.txt, crash.txt, no text; `prof/hw_v26me3/log_run2.txt`, `battery_run2.txt`, `crash_run2.txt`):** stopped between t 2,369 and 2,374 s (battery.txt's last line t 2,369 s, vb 34,112; the log's last line is the window line of vb 34,100), about 39.5 minutes in, in free play past v26me2's stop (vb 33,600-33,700). Exact to its end: 406 / 406 hash lines equal v26's hardware log, 68 / 68 sound checkpoints (ck 0-67) equal the PC's (`prof/mix_ck_x86_free_v26me.txt`). Free play vb 30,600-34,100 (36 windows) against v26: 88.68 ms per step (v26 88.43, +0.25) = logic +0.12, draw +0.05, IOP+sound +0.06, GS +0.02; the ME busy 7.28-7.41 ms per step (93% of an idle ME's polls). Power: USB, external power 0, charging 1 from t 130 s; 70% at the start, 100% from t 1,269 s (4,326 mV at the end); temperature 20 C on every line (unusable on this PSP [I]). Last line: ME on, state 1, heartbeat 30,794,814, 511,018 commands pushed and consumed, 68 checkpoints, no ME exception, power switch 0, parks 0, last power flags 0x000000e4 (battery present, 100%). ME loop 13,416-13,438 iterations per second to t ~1,600 s, 11,331-12,783 later (the busier mix of the codec and free play). **Time, not place:** v26me2 stopped t 2,313-2,324 s (vb 33,600-33,700, on battery at 71%), run 2 t 2,369-2,374 s (vb 34,112 to about 34,150, on USB at 100%): both with the ME mixing, after about the same time but at different points of the game; v26 and v26me (ME not looping) ran their whole 36,500 vblanks. Ruled out: a battery running down (100% on USB), auto sleep (scePowerTick every window), a counter of the game or the mix (vblank, command and heartbeat counts all differ between the two stops), the ring and checkpoint arrays (masked). Open [I]: a crash (to be answered by v26me4's catcher), heat or power from the ME, a system suspend while the ME is taken over (PS2RC's hle_me.h), memory running out. Asked Ffm (17:36) whether it switched off or froze; Ffm (17:47): "switch itself off" (as v26me2).

## Build v26me4 (2026-10-24; build f 17:12 for v26me3 run 1's power-off in the catcher's self-test; build g 17:39 adds battery.txt readings after v26me3 run 2 (read first); build g described to Ffm and delivered)
- **One change against v26me3 build e: the crash catcher's second form** (`psp/v26me4_exc.c` + `psp/v26me4_exc.S` in place of `psp/v26me_exc.*`, which stay as they were; `psp/v26me4.sh`). Registered as before (sctrlHENFindFunction with the same NIDs, `ps2rc_kcall`, handler | 0x80000000; given back before every exit). The handler never returns into the thread: in the exception it stores r0-r31 (the thread's v0 / v1 from COP0 control registers 4 / 5, as PSPLink's psplink/exception_asm.S), Status, LO, HI, BadVAddr, Cause, EPC and control registers 0 / 2 / 3 (the kernel's copies of EPC / Status / Cause), 15 and 16; sets Status &= 0xFFFFFFE4 (kernel mode, exception level cleared, interrupts off: the bits PSPLink clears before its C code); calls `mgs2_exc_screen` on its own 16 KB stack; then spins. A second exception (one in the screen code) spins at once (`mgs2_exc_taken`) and keeps the first record. `mgs2_exc_screen` draws the record with the debug screen's uncached VRAM writes (black, red text): no system call, no gp-relative data, no indirect jump (scratch callwalk.py on build f's disassembly from the handler reaches x_report, code_at, decoded, me_mix_state, me_mix_diag, hle_me_exception, hle_me_power_events and pspDebugScreen* only; nothing flagged; the ME words are read through main RAM's uncached alias). Nothing is written at a crash (the memory stick needs interrupts and threads): the record exists only on the screen, for a photo, and the PSP stays on until the power switch (or the battery) takes it down. v26me3's me_mix_halt (ME back in reset after the record) is not called.
- **The record** (68 x 34 debug screen; at most 29 rows; `psp/v26me4_test.sh` previews it on the PC with the real code and fails on a wrap, a tab, a line over 67 characters or more than 29 rows: self-test 28 rows, widest crash 29, dry run 28): the title ("SELF-TEST: the crash catcher works (expected EPC 08b90398)" when the COP0 or kernel EPC is the self-test's load, else "CRASH: an exception reached the crash catcher"); the sound mix's last vblank and control registers 15 / 16; COP0 EPC / Cause / Status / BadVAddr and the kernel's EPC / Cause / Status, each decoded on the next row (cause name, delay slot, user or kernel, interrupts on or off); 34 registers (r0-r31, lo, hi), 5 a row; the six code words EPC-12..EPC+8 (and the kernel EPC's when different); 42 stack words when sp is in user memory; the EBOOT's code and the overlay arena's range; the Media Engine (mode, heartbeat, state, commands pushed / consumed, checkpoints, idle polls, last command, its exception EPC, the power callback's switch and park counts, last flags); then "Please photograph this screen, then hold the power switch up until the PSP switches off (if it stays on, take the battery out)."
- **Self-test** (cfg `MGS2_EXC=1`): after the start lines the screen asks for X (the test) or O / 15 s (the run). X: the main thread takes battery.txt's semaphore (`me_bat_hold`, `psp/v26me_bat.c` under `-DME_EXC_HOLD`, so no write of it is under way when the PSP stops) and does a misaligned `lw` (`mgs2_exc_test_pc`). Expected: the SELF-TEST record, PSP on. If the PSP switches itself off instead, the registered default handler is not reached on Ffm's PSP [I] and another route is needed (sceKernelRegisterPriorityExceptionHandler or similar) [I]. With the test passed, a later power-off with the catcher on means no exception reached it (power, or an exception the kernel keeps for itself) [I]. On PPSSPP the catcher stays off (no kernel call); `MGS2_EXC_DRY=<vb>` draws a made-up record at that vblank, writes it to crash.txt and log.txt and goes on.
- **Program (build f, /home/claude/v26me4/f, ephemeral; `V26ME=/home/claude/v26me3/e sh psp/v26me4.sh <dir>`):** pads 0x1d60 0x8f4 0x1ddc 0xa8c (4 refits). `tools/dev/elfcmp.py` against v26: 3,017 text symbols +0 (v26's code at v26's addresses), newlib's and libgcc's code moved (modulo 8 KB: _Balloc +0xfc, __adddf3 +0x1d14), rodata, data and bss +0 modulo 8 KB; against v26me3 build e: 3,126 text symbols +0, _Balloc's group -0x3fe0. ovl_arena 0x08bee000 (v26me3 0x08bf8000, v26 0x08bd8000): the overlays are relinked there and differ from v26me3's, so v26me4 ships its own. Handler 0x08b91b18, mgs2_exc_screen 0x08b91538, mgs2_exc_test_pc 0x08b90398 (`lw v0,2(v0)` on x_word 0x0b151bac), mgs2_exc_taken 0x0b151af8, mgs2_exc_regs 0x0b151afc, mgs2_exc_stack 0x0b152cc0, me_bat_hold 0x08b8ff0c, me_bat_unhold 0x08b8ff4c. Earlier builds a-e of v26me4 (first form, stack rows, compact record, interrupts off, semaphore) were never delivered. Two stale comments fixed afterwards (me_mix.c line 80, v26me_bat.c line 104): me_mix.o and v26me_bat.o recompiled with build f's flags are identical to build f's.
- **Emulator (PPSSPP, PPSSPP_VFPU_RN=1, v25f's free route; /home/claude/v26me4/emu; `prof/emu_v26me4/`):** tags f_me1 (`MGS2_SPU_ME=1`, `MGS2_EXC=1`, `MGS2_EXC_DRY=33650`) and f_es2 (`MGS2_SPU_ME=2` simulated, `MGS2_EXC=1`, `MGS2_EXC_DRY=30000`): both 431 / 431 hash lines and 311 / 311 vuh lines equal to v26's emulator log, no split; f_es2 73 / 73 checkpoints eq and equal to the PC's; crash.txt "crash catcher: off (emulator ...)" plus the dry-run record, 28 rows (the code words read PPSSPP's JIT block markers 0x68xxxxxx, as in v26me3).

- **Second change (build g, after v26me3 run 2 stopped 39.5 minutes in): `-DME_DIAG4`** on `psp/v26me_bat.c` (needs `-DME_DIAG3`; `psp/v26me4.sh` compiles it with `-DME_DIAG3 -DME_DIAG4 -DME_EXC_HOLD`). Every battery.txt line ends with "; since power-on N s, CPU N MHz, bus N MHz, break 0xXXXXXXXX, free memory N KB (largest block N KB)": sceKernelGetSystemTimeWide / 1,000,000 (unsigned 64-bit division, __udivdi3, linked already), scePowerGetCpuClockFrequency / scePowerGetBusClockFrequency (imported already by main_psp.c), sbrk(0) (newlib's heap end: a growing break is a growing heap), sceKernelTotalFreeMemSize / sceKernelMaxFreeMemSize >> 10. Reads only. The first try (signed division and the ...Int clock calls) pulled in __divdi3 and two new import stubs and padfit did not settle in 8 refits; the form above links nothing new. Line buffer BAT_LINE 600 (run 2's longest line 352 characters, about 110 added). Only v26me_bat.o differs from build f (v26me4_exc.o, v26me4_exc_s.o, me_mix.o, me_mix_me.o identical).

Catcher: mgs2_exc_handler 0x08b91da4, mgs2_exc_screen 0x08b917c4, mgs2_exc_test_pc 0x08b90624 (`lw v0,2(v0)` on x_word 0x0b151bac), mgs2_exc_taken 0x0b151af8, mgs2_exc_regs 0x0b151afc, mgs2_exc_stack 0x0b152cc0; me_bat_hold 0x08b90198, me_bat_unhold 0x08b901d8. The crash path re-checked on build g's disassembly (callwalk from mgs2_exc_handler): the same functions as build f, no system call, no gp-relative access, no indirect jump.
- **Emulator (build g; `prof/emu_v26me4/*_g_*`):** tags g_me1 (`MGS2_SPU_ME=1`, `MGS2_EXC=1`, `MGS2_EXC_DRY=33650`, 435 s) and g_es2 (`MGS2_SPU_ME=2` simulated, `MGS2_EXC=1`, `MGS2_EXC_DRY=30000`, 440 s): both 431 / 431 hash lines and 311 / 311 vuh lines equal to v26's emulator log, no split; g_es2 73 / 73 checkpoints equal to the PC's; hash, vuh and checkpoint lines identical to build f's runs (only the simulation's "ME Count" suffix differs, the main CPU's cycle counter); crash.txt 28 rows as build f's. Every battery.txt line carries the new fields (g_me1 5,458 lines, g_es2 1,098; PPSSPP's clock skips the battery thread's idle time): CPU 333 MHz, bus 166 MHz, free memory 752-756 KB (largest block 512 KB), break between 0x0b1a2000 and 0x0b43f000 (the heap grows and is trimmed with the disc pack's buffers).

SLES_503.83, mgs2disc.pak, pad.txt, hashref.txt and memcard0 copied from MGS2T_v26me3 on the PC.
- **Self-test on the PSP (Ffm's photo, project chat 18:08): catcher 2 WORKS, and the PSP stayed on.** The record: COP0 EPC 08b90624 = the expected EPC, Cause 10000010 (address error on load or fetch, user, ints on), Status 60008613, BadVAddr 0b151bae; the kernel's copies (cfc0 $0/$2/$3) the same; c15 882ff800, c16 88231c84; v0 0b151bac, ra 08b91d74, gp 08bf0530, k0 0babff00; the word at EPC 8c420002 (`lw v0, 2(v0)`, the deliberate misaligned read); sound mix vb -1 and every ME field 0 (the ME starts after the test, as designed). So a main-CPU exception reaches the catcher on this PSP. Catcher 1 registered the same way (sceKernelRegisterDefaultExceptionHandler, same NID lookup), so it was most likely reached too and its own after-catch path (back into the program, file writes) switched v26me3's PSP off [I].
- **Run (Ffm, files in the thread 2026-10-24 19:24; `prof/hw_v26me4`): it ran to the end, no switch-off, no crash.** Self-test skipped (O), catcher on (handler 08b91da4), crash.txt holds only its two start lines. Free route to vb 36,500 at t 3,062 s on battery (85% -> 56%, 4,098 -> 3,972 mV, external power 0; the temperature reads 20 C on every line), HOME at t 3,093 s. It went through vb 33,600-33,700 (v26me2's stop) and t 2,313-2,374 s (v26me2's and v26me3 run 2's) with nothing unusual in battery.txt (the power callback's flags are 0x80 plus the battery percent). All 431 hash lines (ee + vfb) equal to v26's hashref.txt, 311 / 311 vuh equal to the x86 references, the mix's 73 / 73 checkpoints equal (A = B), ring never full; the ME stopped and was held in reset at the end. Deck steps 6,000, mean 88.97 ms (logic 47.81, draw 33.65, IOP + sound 2.59, GS walk 4.92), max 106.53 ms (v26: 88.72). The ME's Count still reads 0; its idle polls say 7.4-7.8 ms of ME time per step [I]. Reading: the switch-offs are not tied to a game spot or a fixed time [I]; the catcher and the battery log stay in later builds, so a next switch-off shows a crash (record on the screen) or the power [I].

## Hash references from v27 on (2026-10-24 evening; lean_plan.md 1b and 1c)
- **From v27 on, hashref.txt is `prof/hashref_x86_<route>_v27.txt`** (deck, w01a, free; the x86 lines every 100 vblanks: `prof/hashes_x86_<route>_v27.txt`). Builds up to v26me4 keep `_deck_v21` / `_w01a_v21` / `_free_v25f`. Why they change: interrupt handlers and alarms now start with a cleared register file (runtime/kernel.h `k_handler_regs`, Ffm's choice "Clear them", card 2026-10-24 19:24), so the handlers' prologues save zeros instead of the interrupted thread's registers into the interrupt stack in EE RAM: the RAM hash (ee) differs from vb 100 on, the IOP, VFPU-frame (vfb) and VU0 (vuh) hashes do not. `-DK_HKEEP` builds the old behaviour (then the old references apply).
- **Code:** `tools/mk_headers.sh` with `RC_SOUND=1 RC_XUNIT=1 RC_SPECIAL=1 RC_POLL_R= RC_POLL_F= RC_RETLIVE=1` (lean_plan.md 1e, 1b, 1c; with no options it still makes v26's nine headers byte for byte, and with `RC_SOUND=1 RC_XUNIT=1` 1e's). x86 build `tools/dev/x86_ref.sh`, runs `tools/dev/lean/runh.sh`.
- **x86:** against the old references only ee differs; iop and vfb equal on every line (deck and w01a 252 / 252 and 559 / 559, free 365 / 365 and 672 / 672), vuh 311 / 311 on each route.
- **Emulator (2026-10-24 20:15):** a plain PSP build of the same headers (`tools/dev/mk_psp.sh`, no profile or code order; /home/claude/v27/plain, ephemeral) on v24's deck and w01a routes to vb 25,195 and v26's free route to vb 36,495, with the v27 references as hashref.txt: every line equal, no split (deck 370 / 370 ee + vfb, 63 / 63 ee + iop + vfb, 311 / 311 vuh; w01a 370 / 370, 63 / 63, 311 / 311; free 431 / 431, 124 / 124, 311 / 311).

## Free play's hash references from v28 on (2026-10-25; recomp/widths_step2.md "Fixed references and the choice")
- **Free play: `prof/hashref_x86_free_v28.txt` (hashref.txt) and `prof/hashes_x86_free_v28.txt` (every 100 vblanks; every vblank: `prof/hashes_x86_free_v28_every.txt.gz`); deck and w01a keep `_v27`.** v27's free-play references were not the PS2's state: regcache.py made 71 functions 32-bit whose quadword copies through a register need 64 bits (an sq form its WIDE list missed, and frame ld / lq -> sd / sq copies), so P125fd8's matrix copy at vb 33,530 stored lane y as the sign extension of lane x (96 words of effect vertex data for 72 vblanks, and one more VFPU fallback, a NaN). v28's free references differ from v27's only in the vb 33,600 checkpoint's ee hash (EE RAM equal again from vb 33,603) and in the VFPU fallback count of every line from vb 33,600 on (one fewer); iop never differs.
- **Four x86 builds give these references on every checkpoint and per-frame line of the three routes:** the proved widths (hW6, RC_WPW=recomp/widths/wpd_all6.json.gz), v27 with regcache's fixes (RC_CONDCLEAN=1 RC_SQWIDE=1), the narrow-in hybrid (hyb7) and an all-64-bit build (RC_WIDE=1: no width decided at all; its coverage list adds the 326 ELF functions v27 inlines, as RC_WIDE turns the ELF's inlining off). All four equal v27's references on the deck and w01a.
- Builds up to v27 keep the `_v27` free references (their code has the copy as v27 made it).
